            </activation>

            </modules>

                                </goals>

                                    <executable>docker</executable>
                                    <workingDirectory>${project.build.directory}</workingDirectory>

                                        <argument>buildx</argument>

                                        <argument>.</argument>
                                    </arguments>
                                </configuration>
                            </execution>

                                <id>push-version-docker-amd-arm-images</id>

                                    <goal>exec</goal>
                                </goals>
                                <configuration>
                                    <executable>docker</executable>
                                    <workingDirectory>${project.build.directory}</workingDirectory>
                                    <arguments>
                                        <argument>buildx</argument>
